EVIDENCE-SUPPORTED PREMISE ADMISSION V2

Authoritative replay: verified-v2-replay/. Other replay directories are intermediate development snapshots; use verified-v2-replay/results.json for final evidence. No historical artifacts are rewritten.

ARCHITECTURE
Validated canonical source context → existing known compatibility path when covered → otherwise independent evidence-supported factors → explicit admission result/provenance → Boolean projection for existing preselection.
Admission is separate from Naturalness/SFE/BG/ranking scores. No global creative score is introduced. Naturalness reports/metrics and their legacy compatibility grades are retained; admissionV2 is the authoritative new decision. legacyAdmitted distinguishes the old Boolean from its new projection.
Existing runtime code is unchanged. The new evaluator is app/workflow/evidence_supported_admission_v2.py; the explicit offline CLI selects it. No legacy API/default behavior is switched implicitly.

STATUS / PROVENANCE MODEL
Coverage: KNOWN_TAXONOMY_SUPPORTED / EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED_UNCLASSIFIED / UNSUPPORTED / INCOMPATIBLE. Coverage here means admission evidence, not existence of a behavior.
Status: ADMITTED_KNOWN_COMPATIBILITY / ADMITTED_EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED_UNCLASSIFIED / NOT_ADMITTED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE / NOT_ADMITTED_INCOMPATIBLE / NOT_ADMITTED_ARBITRARY / NOT_ADMITTED_OPENING_WEAK / NOT_ADMITTED_RULE_INCOHERENT.
Provenance: KNOWN_COMPATIBILITY / EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED_UNCLASSIFIED / NONE. Rejected fallback evaluations also retain their route provenance; that is not an admission claim. Response class/status stay separate: an admitted unknown behavior has responseClassification=null and PRESENT_UNCLASSIFIED, never an invented class.

KNOWN PATH
Existing object/response compatibility and admission are called unchanged. Known covered incompatible pairs are returned immediately as NOT_ADMITTED_INCOMPATIBLE and cannot enter fallback. Existing deterministic known-path Boolean outcomes are identical, including cases with a separately weak opening.
Classified-but-uncovered object/compatibility gaps can enter fallback. A known label is not sufficient, and absence of a label is not a rejection criterion. The clock minimal fixture proves a classified RETURN_ORIGIN gap can use the same fallback without changing its class.

EVIDENCE-SUPPORTED PATH
All core factors must be supported: normal expectation, explicit visible response, exact-object functional dependency, concrete trigger, DIRECT goal interference, source-rule coherence, non-arbitrary behavior and functional/affordance relation.
Exact-object evidence is triangulated: non-identity function operands must link expectation/rule/trigger and at least two primary affected consequence subjects; whyThisObject alone cannot pass. Sharing the same affected noun is insufficient: a normal-function or goal-function operation must also be grounded in the declared rule. A covered existing object role corroborates the ordinary function. A source short name may be anchored across fields without creating an object alias/taxonomy row.
Coherence additionally checks explicit source constraints: opposite declared outcomes, undeclared exclusive actor/trigger restrictions, asserted response-model changes, fixed timing changes, unrelated agency/material/functions, invisible causal machinery and explicitly independent rules. Every rejection carries exact quoted source and its declared rule.
Clarification/negated alternatives are not violations; a simile is not proof of a literal material transformation. These safeguards have explicit regression tests. The evaluator performs deterministic source comparisons, not complete semantic/physical proof. Absence of detected contradiction is not a theorem; source admission provenance and review notes remain visible for manual review.

OPENING REMAINS INDEPENDENT AND AUTHORITATIVE
The main replay projects the premise-level decision with opening_mandatory=False, matching the existing separation of premise and production gates. Every decision still exposes openingPassed, the opening factor/score and productionOpeningRequirementSatisfied.
opening_mandatory=True produces the stricter combined decision; a coherent unknown premise with opening35 becomes NOT_ADMITTED_OPENING_WEAK under that policy. Existing production preselection always retains its unchanged opening<60 gate, regardless of which Boolean is projected. No source can become production-admissible through weak opening.
raw013: core premise admitted; combined opening-mandatory decision NOT_ADMITTED_OPENING_WEAK; unchanged production gate blocks it. raw026/raw027: strong opening90 but source-rule contradictions prevent core admission.

Thirteen are NOT_ADMITTED_RULE_INCOHERENT; four lack sufficient grounded functional/goal/coherence evidence. Insufficient literal grounding is not proof of a poor idea or absent response.
Naturalness scalar metrics, all opening scores, entire SFE reports, entire BG reports and entire ranking reports remain unchanged. Production-admissible0; slate0.

Scores, findings and evaluator sources are not edited. The per-row legacyDiagnosticOrderingDifference records it; this is not a scoring delta.
